The one-marked tangent monad uses the zero-anchor tangent coefficient
\[
    z(M)=\left(M\xrightarrow0\mathbb T_{B/R}^{\mathrm{pc}}[1]\right).
\]
The linearization theorem expands every multilinear operation around the fixed
algebra and uses one-variable stabilization: terms containing exactly one
tangent input survive, while terms containing at least two tangent inputs have
zero linearization.  It does not assert that all operations of arity at least
two vanish.

Coefficient connections are first-weight partial actions.  At weight $r$, the
new indecomposable datum is the weight-$r$ one-marked corolla.  Proper
weight-$r$ decompositions form its punctured latching category and are
evaluable from the lower marked-path actions together with the fixed
algebroid structure.  The corolla--latching extension theorem identifies
extension through weight $r$ with a filler of
\[
    L_r(\mathfrak a;M)\longrightarrow C_r(\mathfrak a;M),
\]
whose cofiber defines the obstruction coefficient.  No false claim that
$F_{\le r}\mathbb U$ is obtained from $F_{\le r-1}\mathbb U$ by adjoining a
weight-$r$ corolla is used.  The Bianchi identity is instead the next-weight
restriction of the universal monad-defect four-term syzygy; for morphisms it
is the corresponding three-term morphism-defect syzygy.  These identities,
not a fixed-weight $d^2$ argument, supply the coherent higher Bianchi
hierarchy.

The finite-order operator theory is constructed from completed adic principal
parts with explicit source and target actions.  The dense two-sided kernel
category requires the two restrictions to be almost perfect, not connective
almost perfect, so that it is genuinely stable.  Connectivity may be restored
by finite shifts inside the convolution proof.  Finite diagonal order is
additive under convolution, and the continuous source duals generate the
pro-coherent two-sided kernel category
$\operatorname{Kern}^{\vee}_{B/R}$.  This kernel category acts on the
one-sided pro-coherent coefficient category
$\operatorname{QC}^{\vee}_B$.  The regular coefficient $B$ is a module for
the Artinian distribution algebra in that one-sided category by continuous
dualization of the source structural map; it is not a module object in the
two-sided kernel category and no algebra augmentation to $B$ is inferred.
Continuous source coduality of the free distribution-module bar recovers the
completed Artinian Cech terms degree-by-degree, using the theorem that the
completed $n$-simplex is the completed $n$-fold tensor power of the
degree-one Cech coring.  Artinian Koszul-dual cells are dense by the
preceding chapter's cellular recognition theorem, so the controller-level
kernel algebra is a presentation-free left Kan extension.

The normalized one-marked monadic bar is exhaustively filtered, not declared
complete.  The Maurer--Cartan convolution object
\[
    \operatorname{map}_{
        \operatorname{Ch}_{\geq0}(\operatorname{QC}^{\vee}_B)}
    \left(
        \overline{\mathbb B}^{\mathrm{Sp}}_{\mathfrak a}(M),
        M[0]
    \right)
\]
is canonically complete because mapping out of the exhaustive filtered
colimit in the normalized chain category is an inverse limit.  On the rational dg comparison scope, admissible one-marked spinal-cut
cooperations construct the convolution $L_\infty$ operations.  The canonical
Maurer--Cartan twisting element is the suspended positive-corolla
corestriction of the representation action; it is not the degree-zero
normalized monadic bar augmentation.  Their filtration is additive, so the
operations extend to this inverse-limit completion.  The strict and
transferred Maurer--Cartan expressions therefore live in a constructed
complete algebraic object rather than in an undeclared pronilpotent
completion.

\subsection{Rational comparison scope}

In the rational dg range the chapter constructs the dg representation and
bimodule enveloping-algebra comparison, the intrinsic pro-coherent symbol, the
cellwise PBW--Spencer morphism, the degreewise completed Cech filtration,
the constructed completed formal-leaf Spencer object, the
relative $R$-linear Cech--distribution bar duality, the higher Cech cotangent formula
\[
    L_{B/P_A^{[n]}}[-1]
    \simeq
    L_{B/A}^{\oplus n},
\]
the cellwise CE--Spencer comparison map, the canonical Cartan
Lie-derivative representation on CE cochains, the rooted-tree convolution
$L_\infty$ structure, and the strict and transferred Maurer--Cartan formulas
in the constructed convolution completion.

The ordinary symmetric formula
\[
    \left(
        \operatorname{Sym}^rL
    \right)^\vee
    \simeq
    \operatorname{Sym}^r(L^\vee)
\]
is stated only when $L$ is perfect.  The cellwise ordinary PBW--Spencer and
distribution-module-mediated underlying CE--Spencer equivalences are stated on their explicit
dg model range.  The controller-level ordinary PBW equivalence is obtained
when the perfect dg cells form a cofinal subdiagram of the canonical Artinian
density diagram.  Independently, the controller-level CE--Spencer
equivalence is constructed by stabilized module Koszul duality and the proved
compact-cell density of coherent square-zero tangent generators on the stated
dg representation range; it requires no perfect-cell cofinality.  Cofinality
is therefore a property appearing only in the ordinary PBW theorem
hypothesis; no ``finite-duality'', ``PBW-admissible'', or ``CE-admissible''
structure is attached to a controller.

\subsection{No axiom disguised as datum, presentation, admissibility, or package}

None of the following is input data of a brave-new formal Lie groupoid, its
spectral differentiation, or its Atiyah--Spencer comparison:
\begin{enumerate}
    \item a finite infinitesimal factorization or Artinian square-zero chain;
    \item a finite-order jet tower, numerical jet order, or reduced-jet
    decomposition;
    \item a chosen Cech, comonadic-cobar, Spencer, principal-parts, PBW, CE,
    or enveloping presentation;
    \item an adic ideal generator, topology, filtration, or completion
    presentation;
    \item a raw geometric differential or PD substitution law on the
    separated marked-word sector;
    \item a chosen family of operation inputs or a tautological
    multi-derivation tuple;
    \item a scalar-Hurewicz equivalence;
    \item an identification of a reduced scalar cross-effect with its
    cotangent primitive;
    \item a representation-recognition structure or operator-algebra
    augmentation;
    \item an admissibility structure or admissible-algebroid class;
    \item a ``finite-duality'', PBW, or CE package;
    \item a chosen nonlinear, stable, partition-Lie, or dg anchor;
    \item an isotropy-exactness, primitive-exactness, transitivity, or
    formalized-adjoint-exactness structure;
    \item an ambient
    $\mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}$-representation on the independent
    actual-isotropy controller;
    \item a Cartan coaction on an arbitrary coefficient;
    \item a connection, splitting, curvature, Bianchi datum, flatness datum,
    or pronilpotence datum;
    \item a formal-moduli recognition datum, chosen affine-etale basis, or
    independently retained descent datum;
    \item an identification of raw geometric, scalar-dual, and
    Artinian-formalized marked cells;
    \item a choice identifying the source and target $B$-actions of a
    principal-parts kernel;
    \item an integral spectral-to-animated comparison.
\end{enumerate}

All constructions used in place of these potential packages are functorial:
ordinal-sum decalage, support transport, directional square-zero cubes, finite
total cofibres and fibres, scalar dualization, relative Hurewicz morphisms,
comonadic scalar-diagonal bars, cotangent primitives, structured square-zero
pullbacks, stable localization, monadic and comonadic constructions,
multi-filtered square-zero geometry, corolla latching, monad-defect and
morphism-defect syzygies, stable normalization, adic and multi-filtration,
continuous duality, two-sided kernel convolution, mapping-object completion,
rooted-tree convolution, and left Kan extension.

\subsection{Theorem-domain hypotheses}

The following remain genuine domains of proved constructions rather than
fields of structure:
\begin{enumerate}
    \item representability where quasi-coherent cotangent,
    principal-parts, or ordinary tangent geometry is invoked;
    \item coherence of spectral coefficient rings for pro-coherent Koszul
    duality;
    \item local coherence and qcqs for the global coherent etale coefficient
    system;
    \item connectivity and almost-perfectness in the filtered cotangent and
    continuous-duality arguments on their stated dense sectors;
    \item spectral etaleness for the varying-object global functoriality
    presently constructed;
    \item rationality for dg rectification;
    \item the coefficient and boundedness/model range of the dg
    Lie-algebroid representation theorem;
    \item perfectness of $L_{B/A}$ only where the ordinary symmetric dual
    formula is used;
    \item explicit cofinality of perfect dg cells only for the
    controller-level ordinary PBW equivalence.
\end{enumerate}
None is retained as a datum on $\mathfrak G$ or its controller.

\subsection{Still deferred}

The chapter does not construct a native integration functor from an arbitrary
abstract spectral partition-Lie algebroid to a native fixed-object formal Lie
groupoid.  It does not identify
$\mathfrak l_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{sp}}$ with
$\mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}$ integrally.  It does not prove every
raw-to-formalized marked relation map invertible.  It does not identify the
formalized stable adjoint representation coefficient with the independent
actual-isotropy controller.  It does not construct an integral
spectral-to-animated Artinian comparison.  Global Lie-groupoid integrability
beyond formal integration remains separate.

\section{The Atiyah--Spencer higher-operation package}
\label{sec:as-synthesis}

\begin{theorem}[Atiyah--Spencer and Higher Brave-New Lie Package]
\label{thm:as-package}
Let
\[
    \mathfrak G_\bullet
    \in
    \operatorname{FormLieGpd}(X/S).
\]
The chapter canonically constructs:
\begin{enumerate}
    \item the iterated jet endpoint correspondences;

    \item geometric scalar Cech cochains, correctly indexed Cartan and cofree
    cobars, the canonical extra-codegeneracy splitting, and the ordinal-sum
    source-fixed decalage theorem and its arbitrary-map extension;

    \item stable Spencer normalization and complete partial-totalization
    filtrations;

    \item the spectral co-Kleisli category and universal cofree Spencer action;

    \item first-jet Atiyah extensions, represented principal parts, and
    first-order scalar cotangent linearization;

    \item the formalized relation, the covariant raw-to-formalized relation
    map, contravariant formalized-to-raw jet/cobar maps, scalar restriction,
    and stable discrepancies;

    \item tangent representation categories, relative stable Schlessinger
    reflection, explicit universal Artinian sections, and correctly typed
    formalized scalar Spencer cochain representations;

    \item affine tangent enveloping monads and their coherent spectral-etale
    descent to the global tangent enveloping monad;

    \item the descended nonlinear adjoint group, its formalization by
    dependent right direct image, the counit comparison
    \[
        I_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Ad,fm}}
        \longrightarrow
        I_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{for}},
    \]
    and the formalized differentiated adjoint representation, without an
    unconditional identification with
    $\mathfrak i_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}$;

    \item the Cech diagonal primitive and bar--cotangent equivalence
    \[
        \operatorname{cot}_{B/R}^{\mathrm{Sp}}(A)
        \xrightarrow{\sim}
        \operatorname{cot}_{B/R}(A);
    \]

    \item the transported Artinian Spencer coLie comonad
    \[
        \operatorname{coSp}_{B/R}^{\mathrm{Art}}
        =
        \operatorname{cot}^{\mathrm{Sp}}_{B/R}
        \operatorname{sqz}_{B/R},
    \]
    whose counit is transported from the actual cotangent--square-zero
    adjunction and whose Cech-unit primitive cooperation is proved to be its
    comultiplication, together with the comonad/monad equivalences
    \[
        \operatorname{coSp}_{B/R}^{\mathrm{Art}}
        \xrightarrow{\sim}
        \operatorname{coLie}_{B/R}^{\pi,E_\infty},
        \qquad
        \operatorname{Lie}^{\mathrm{Sp,Art}}_{B/R}
        \xrightarrow{\sim}
        \operatorname{Lie}^{\pi,E_\infty}_{B/R};
    \]

    \item the global integral controller comparison
    \[
    \boxed{
        \Xi_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Art}}:
        \mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}
        \xrightarrow{\sim}
        \mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Sp,Art}};
    }
    \]

    \item separated singleton-marked cells and face-closed support-marked
    raw/formalized Cech systems, with normalized differential, symmetric
    relabelling, and geometric discrepancies;

    \item the support-marked scalar dual of formalized geometry, the canonical
    Artinian scalar restriction along
    $\beta_{\widetilde q}$, the reduced support scalar Artinian bar
    $\mathbf{RedBar}_{A/B}(I)$, and its source-fixed internally desuspended
    primitive form $\mathbf{RedBar}^{\mathrm{prim}}_{A/B}(I)$, retained as a
    geometric scalar branch;

    \item the directional Artinian square-zero cube $J\mapsto A_J$, its
    source-fixed directional cross-effect
    $\mathbf{DirRedBar}^{\mathrm{prim}}_{A_I/B}(I)$, and the theorem
    identifying it with the all-positive multiweight of the single Cech
    primitive;

    \item finite multi-filtered square-zero systems, the unrestricted
    multi-filtered cotangent--square-zero adjunction and comonad $G_I$,
    tail-connectivity and rectangular multicompleteness, symmetric multi-input
    operation functors, and the integral PD cooperation substitution system;

    \item the canonical comonadic scalar-diagonal bar, the correctly indexed
    cofree cotangent cobar, and the genuine normalized comparison
    \[
        h_I^{\mathrm{comon}}:
        \mathbf{ScCotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I)
        \longrightarrow
        \mathbf{CotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I),
    \]
    whose augmented degree is the exact scalar Hurewicz--diagonal comparison
    and which is not declared an equivalence;

    \item the primitive cotangent cobar and the correctly typed totalization
    comparison
    \[
    \boxed{
    \begin{aligned}
        \operatorname{Tot}_{\mathrm{DK}}\mathbf{CotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I)
        &\xrightarrow{\sim}
        \operatorname{CotPrim}^{\mathrm{Sp}}_{B/R}(I;-)\\
        &\xrightarrow{\sim}
        \operatorname{coOp}^{\mathrm{Sp}}_{B/R}(I;-),
    \end{aligned}}
    \]
    where the second equivalence is the single-diagonal multi-filtered
    cotangent theorem;

    \item the unrestricted filtered cotangent--square-zero adjunction, proved
    completeness and tail connectivity of anchor-weight objects, the
    exhaustive arity-resolved partition-Lie filtration, and the additive
    one-marked tangent filtration;

    \item the zero-anchor tangent coefficient $z(M)$, the one-marked
    linearization theorem for multilinear operations, and the equivalence of
    the one-marked relative bar monad with the tangent enveloping monad;

    \item coefficient connections, explicit rooted-tree corolla and latching
    objects, the corolla--latching extension theorem, curvature, the complete
    flatness obstruction tower, the universal monad-defect Bianchi syzygy,
    unconditional Bianchi coherence, and the higher Bianchi hierarchy;

    \item the parallel corolla--latching obstruction tower for algebroid
    morphisms and its anchor-fibre factorization consequence;

    \item strong monoidality of reduced stabilization, the first differential
    of inversion, the right-normalized nonlinear-isotropy commutator, and its
    integral mixed \emph{cotangent} primitive comparison with the binary
    operation of differentiated actual isotropy;

    \item completed adic principal parts, source/target-correct finite-order
    Spencer operators, the stable almost-perfect finite-diagonal two-sided
    kernel category and its convolution, the one-sided kernel action on
    pro-coherent coefficients, continuous source coduality, intrinsic
    pro-coherent symbols, filtered distribution kernel algebras, the iterated
    completed Cech-coring theorem, the distribution free-module-bar
    reconstruction of completed Artinian Spencer cochains, and Artinian-cell
    density/left-Kan reconstruction;

    \item the raw-to-formalized co-Kleisli map and the integral global tangent
    enveloping action on formalized Spencer cochains;

    \item complete formalized/raw Cartan transport and the normalized integral
    monadic Spencer twisting system;

    \item the exhaustive one-marked bar filtration and the canonically complete
    Maurer--Cartan convolution object obtained by mapping out of that
    exhaustive bar;

    \item on the explicit rational dg comparison scope, the rooted-tree
    convolution $L_\infty$ operations, their additive Maurer--Cartan
    filtration, the canonical suspended-corolla degree-one twisting element,
    and the strict and transferred Maurer--Cartan equations in the constructed
    completion;

    \item in explicit rational dg ranges, the bimodule enveloping-algebra
    representation, intrinsic pro-coherent symbol, ordinary symmetric symbol
    under perfectness, cellwise PBW--Spencer equivalence, higher Cech
    cotangent calculation, cellwise CE--Spencer comparison, the
    controller-level ordinary PBW comparison under explicit perfect-cell
    cofinality, and the controller-level CE--Spencer comparison by stabilized
    module Koszul duality and compact tangent-cell density on the dg
    representation scope;

    \item one-object, action, gauge, and ordinary smooth specializations,
    including coefficient connections, anchor-splitting curvature, faithful
    first-principal-parts symbols, direct recovery of the classical
    right-invariant bracket, and its controller comparison on the represented
    effective-quotient dg scope;

    \item fixed-object functoriality with the correct relation/jet variance,
    arbitrary base change of raw object-level constructions, canonical
    base-change maps on external co-Kleisli mapping spectra, spectral-etale
    varying-object functoriality of the formalized algebraic package, and
    spectral-etale descent.
\end{enumerate}

No connection, splitting, curvature, Bianchi coherence, Artinian chain,
jet-order presentation, Spencer presentation, comonadic-bar presentation,
enveloping presentation, operator-algebra augmentation, admissibility
structure, arbitrary Cartan coaction, recognition datum, raw PD operad
structure, chosen operation input tuple, scalar-Hurewicz equivalence,
pronilpotence datum, chosen source/target-kernel identification, or ambient
representation on the independent actual-isotropy controller is part of the
input.

The precise operation architecture is the pair of ladders
\[
\boxed{
\begin{aligned}
\mathbb B_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Sp,raw}}(I)
&\xrightarrow{\ \Lambda_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Sp}}(I)\ }
\mathbb B_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Sp,fm}}(I),\\[2mm]
\mathscr B^{\mathrm{supp,fm},\bullet}_{\mathfrak G,\mathcal O}(I)
&\xrightarrow{\ \operatorname{res}^{\mathrm{supp}}_{\widetilde q}\ }
\mathscr R_{A/B}^{\bullet}(I)
\longrightarrow
\mathbf{RedBar}^{\mathrm{prim}}_{A/B}(I),
\end{aligned}}
\]
and
\[
\boxed{
\begin{aligned}
\left(\mathbf{DirRedBar}^{\mathrm{prim}}_{A_I/B}(I)\right)^0
&\longrightarrow
\mathsf{ScCotCobar}^{-1}_{B/R}(I;D_I),\\[2mm]
\mathbf{ScCotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I)
&\xrightarrow{\ h_I^{\mathrm{comon}}\ }
\mathbf{CotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I),\\[2mm]
\operatorname{Tot}_{\mathrm{DK}}\mathbf{CotBar}_{B/R}(I;D_I)
&\xrightarrow{\sim}
\operatorname{CotPrim}^{\mathrm{Sp}}_{B/R}(I;-)\\
&\xrightarrow{\sim}
\operatorname{coOp}_{B/R}^{\mathrm{Sp}}(I;-)
\xrightarrow{\sim}
\operatorname{coOp}_{B/R}^{\pi,E_\infty}(I;-)
\xrightarrow{\ \vee\ }
\text{spectral partition-Lie operations}.
\end{aligned}}
\]
The first display is the geometric support-marked branch.  The second is the
directional Artinian branch.  The Cech and comonadic cosimplicial directions
meet at the exact scalar diagonal but are not identified.  The normalized
scalar-to-cotangent map is a comparison, not an equivalence; the equivalences
begin only after totalization of the primitive cotangent cobar.  No raw
Artinian primitive object is asserted without a raw Artinian lift.

The adjoint comparison is independently
\[
\boxed{
    I_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Ad,fm}}
    \longrightarrow
    I_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{for}},
}
\]
with a corresponding stable coefficient comparison.  It becomes an
equivalence when quotient formalization is an equivalence; no unconditional
comparison with the spectral actual-isotropy controller is inserted.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Every item is produced by a functor, adjunction, localization, stabilization,
finite-limit or finite-colimit construction, canonical monadic/comonadic
construction, directional square-zero cross-effect, comonadic scalar-diagonal
bar construction, filtered or multi-filtered cotangent calculation, corolla
latching, monad-defect or morphism-defect syzygy, relative Hurewicz morphism,
continuous duality, two-sided kernel convolution, one-sided kernel module
action, relative $R$-linear continuous source coduality, normalized-chain
mapping-object completion,
rooted-tree convolution, or left Kan extension
whose hypotheses are proved in the preceding sections.  The variance of
raw/formalized maps is fixed by the quotient adjunction, and the geometric,
scalar, cotangent, formal-moduli, adjoint, and actual-isotropy levels are never
identified merely by notation.  No additional field of structure is
introduced in the synthesis.
\end{proof}

\section{Handoff to brave-new Lie integration}
\label{sec:as-handoff-integration}

The brave-new Lie arc now has three completed stages.

The formal-groupoid chapter constructs the nonlinear fixed-object formal
groupoid
\[
    \mathfrak G_\bullet
\]
together with its quotient, actual isotropy, descended adjoint group and
transport, disk--jet calculus, and nonlinear commutator.

The differentiation chapter constructs the Artinian-formalized spectral
controller
\[
    \mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}
\]
and its rational dg image.

The present chapter constructs the operation comparison without identifying
coefficient categories by fiat.  It constructs raw and formalized
support-marked Cech geometry and their stable discrepancies; the scalar dual
of formalized support-marked geometry; the canonical Artinian Cech test map;
reduced and source-fixed primitive support scalar Artinian bars; directional
Artinian square-zero cross-effects; the relative Hurewicz morphism; the
canonical comonadic scalar-diagonal bar and its comparison with the correctly
indexed cotangent cobar; the multi-filtered cotangent--square-zero comonad;
single-diagonal multi-filtered cotangent primitives; the complete multiweight
PD cooperation system; and the integral equivalence
\[
    \mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\mathrm{Sp,Art}}
    \simeq
    \mathbb A_{\mathfrak G}^{\pi,E_\infty}.
\]
The scalar comparison remains a comparison map; the equivalences begin only
after totalization of the independently constructed primitive cotangent
cobar.

The chapter also constructs the tangent representation calculus and the
zero-anchor one-marked derivative of the partition-Lie operation system; the
global tangent monad and its additive weight filtration; the corolla--latching
extension theorem for coefficient actions and algebroid morphisms; curvature,
Bianchi, and the complete obstruction towers; finite-order two-sided
principal-parts kernels and their stable almost-perfect pro-coherent
convolution category; its one-sided action on pro-coherent coefficients,
continuous source coduality, the iterated completed Cech-coring theorem, and
the distribution free-module-bar reconstruction of completed Artinian
Spencer cochains; the formalized adjoint representation and its comparison to
actual nonlinear isotropy; and the explicitly scoped rational PBW, CE, and
Maurer--Cartan comparisons, including compact tangent-cell density for the
controller-level CE theorem.  The infinite transferred Maurer--Cartan series is
formed in the canonically complete convolution object obtained by mapping out
of the exhaustive one-marked bar, and the rational convolution $L_\infty$
operations are constructed from the rooted-tree cut cooperations of that bar.

The remaining problem is native integration.  Affine spectral formal-moduli
integration is already inverse to affine differentiation.  What remains is to
reconstruct from an abstract spectral partition-Lie algebroid a native
fixed-object formal groupoid and compare
\[
\boxed{
    \operatorname{Int}^{\mathrm{native}}
    \circ
    \operatorname{Diff}^{\pi,E_\infty},
    \qquad
    \operatorname{Diff}^{\pi,E_\infty}
    \circ
    \operatorname{Int}^{\mathrm{native}}.
}
\]
The native comparison must determine how the reconstructed quotient maps to
its Artinian formalization, control the resulting raw/formalized geometric
marked discrepancies, compare native adjoint/isotropy constructions with
their formalized tangent images, compare one-object native integration with
the independent Hopf-derived-primitives construction, and relate formal
integration to global Lie-groupoid integrability.

The four-stage architecture is
\[
\boxed{
\text{formal groupoids}
\longrightarrow
\text{differentiation}
\longrightarrow
\text{Atiyah--Spencer operations}
\longrightarrow
\text{native integration and comparison}.
}
\]
